game-api: tutorials.ts §

Declarations from tutorials.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/tutorials.ts.

TutorialValues §

Read-only scalar telemetry map observed by tutorial runners (e.g. `flying`, `landed`, `coasting`, `safe`, `bound`, `bodyId`, `clearance` in metres, `radius` in metres, `radiusRatio`, `speed` in m/s, `periapsis` and `apoapsis` in metres, `periapsisRatio` and `apoapsisRatio`, `eccentricity`, `energy` in J/kg, `angularMomentum` in m²/s, `period` in seconds, `nodeCount`, `nodeDv` in m/s, and `alarmCount`). Never writes simulation state.

export type TutorialValues = Readonly<Record<string, number | string | boolean>>;

Used by: spacelab.tutorial-runner.

TutorialObservation §

Detached read-only observation snapshot passed to a tutorial runner at start and on each update. Tutorials are read-only observers: they read scalar telemetry and never mutate simulation state.

export interface TutorialObservation {
  /** Session, target and revision/teleport continuity. Changing it invalidates an attempt. */
  readonly identity: object;
  /** Monotonic simulation time in seconds. Rewinds (`time < lastTime`) or non-finite values interrupt the active attempt. */
  readonly time: number;
  /** Whether the simulation is currently paused; paused frames do not advance timed holds. */
  readonly paused: boolean;
  /** Read-only scalar telemetry fields evaluated against step conditions. */
  readonly values: TutorialValues;
}

Used by: spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

TutorialCondition §

Single scalar predicate evaluated by a `TutorialRunner` against `TutorialObservation.values` and the lesson-start baseline. Non-finite numbers or missing fields evaluate to `false`; `'ratio-gte'` and `'ratio-lte'` require a finite positive baseline.

export interface TutorialCondition {
  /** Name of the scalar key in `TutorialValues` to inspect. */
  readonly field: string;
  /** Comparison operator: value threshold (`'gte'`, `'lte'`, `'eq'`), change from baseline (`'changed'`), or ratio to baseline (`'ratio-gte'`, `'ratio-lte'`). */
  readonly operator: 'gte' | 'lte' | 'eq' | 'changed' | 'ratio-gte' | 'ratio-lte';
  /** Target threshold or exact match value (omitted for `'changed'`). */
  readonly value?: number | string | boolean;
}

Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

TutorialStep §

One sequential objective within a `TutorialLesson`: either simulation-checked `conditions` (up to 32) or a multiple-choice `question`, never both.

export interface TutorialStep {
  /** Short step heading shown in the objective banner and HUD button. */
  readonly title: string;
  /** Actionable player instruction explaining what to fly, observe or answer. */
  readonly instruction: string;
  /** Optional guidance revealed when the learner opens the hint disclosure. */
  readonly hint: string;
  /** Read-only scalar predicates (1 to 32) that must all hold simultaneously. Mutually exclusive with `question`. */
  readonly conditions?: readonly TutorialCondition[];
  /** UI/planning objectives may complete while paused; they cannot have a timed hold. */
  readonly allowPaused?: boolean;
  /** Consecutive simulation seconds; paused frames never accrue credit. */
  readonly holdSeconds?: number;
  /** Multiple-choice knowledge check (mutually exclusive with `conditions`); `correct` is the 0-based index in `choices`. */
  readonly question?: { readonly choices: readonly string[]; readonly correct: number; readonly explanation: string };
}

Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons.

TutorialLesson §

Read-only definition of a multi-step tutorial lesson (1 to 64 steps). Versioned so changes to completion criteria invalidate stale completion records (`<id>@<version>`).

export interface TutorialLesson {
  /** Stable lesson identifier within a course. */
  readonly id: string;
  /** Positive integer version encoded in persisted completion keys (`<id>@<version>`). */
  readonly version: number;
  /** Human-readable lesson title. */
  readonly title: string;
  /** Difficulty tier used to group lessons in the selector. */
  readonly level: 'First flights' | 'Orbital skills' | 'Advanced';
  /** Introductory summary shown above the objective. */
  readonly introduction: string;
  /** Expandable physics and orbital mechanics notes ("Go deeper"). */
  readonly deeper: string;
  /** Ordered sequence of 1 to 64 checked objectives or knowledge questions. */
  readonly steps: readonly TutorialStep[];
}

Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

TutorialRunner §

Stateful read-only state machine evaluating a `TutorialLesson` against `TutorialObservation` snapshots and persisting versioned completion keys (`<lesson.id>@<lesson.version>`, up to 256 entries). Never mutates simulation state, DOM nodes or timers. Caller owns and disposes the runner.

export interface TutorialRunner extends Disposable {
  /** Currently active lesson, or `undefined` when idle or stopped. */
  readonly lesson: TutorialLesson | undefined;
  /** Zero-based index of the current step in `lesson.steps`. */
  readonly step: number;
  /** `true` once all steps of `lesson` have been completed. */
  readonly finished: boolean;
  /** `true` if `observation.identity` changed or `observation.time` rewound or became non-finite during an active lesson. */
  readonly interrupted: boolean;
  /** Latest learner-facing status or explanation message. */
  readonly feedback: string;
  /** Read-only set of completed `<lesson.id>@<lesson.version>` keys loaded from storage and updated on lesson completion. */
  readonly completed: ReadonlySet<string>;
  /**
   * Validates `lesson` (1–64 steps, each with either 1–32 conditions or a valid question, non-negative finite `holdSeconds`,
   * and no `holdSeconds > 0` when `allowPaused` is set) and starts tracking from `observation` as the baseline.
   * Throws if disposed or if the lesson or observation time is invalid.
   */
  start(lesson: TutorialLesson, observation: TutorialObservation): void;
  /**
   * Evaluates the current step's conditions against `observation`. Interrupts the attempt if `identity` changed or simulation
   * `time` decreased. Advances the step once conditions hold for `holdSeconds` consecutive unpaused simulation seconds.
   */
  update(observation: TutorialObservation): void;
  /**
   * Submits a 0-based `choice` index for the current step's knowledge question.
   * Returns `true` and advances the step when `choice === question.correct`; returns `false` otherwise or when inactive.
   */
  answer(choice: number): boolean;
  /** Clears the active lesson, interruption state and hold timer while preserving `completed` progress. */
  stop(): void;
}

Used by: spacelab.tutorial-runner.

TutorialRunnerFactory §

Stateless factory creating independent `TutorialRunner` state machines (`learning.runner@1.0.0`).

export interface TutorialRunnerFactory {
  /**
   * Creates a runner backed by optional `storage` under `storageKey` (recommended format: `<courseId>/v<courseVersion>/<learnerKey>`).
   * Storage read/write failures fall back silently to in-memory tracking.
   */
  create(options: { readonly storage?: ThemePreferences; readonly storageKey: string }): TutorialRunner;
}

Used by: spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

TUTORIAL_RUNNERS §

Service key for the reusable tutorial runner factory (`learning.runner@1.0.0`).

export const TUTORIAL_RUNNERS: ServiceKey<TutorialRunnerFactory> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'learning.runner', version: '1.0.0' });

Used by: spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

AcademyPractice §

Declarative practice ship placement requested only by an explicit learner button action. Ratios are measured from the body's reference radius; no world physics is changed.

export interface AcademyPractice {
  /** Placement archetype: launch pad respawn (`'pad'`), bound orbit (`'circle'`, `'ellipse'`), hyperbolic lunar approach (`'approach'`), or moon transfer (`'encounter'`). */
  readonly kind: 'pad' | 'circle' | 'ellipse' | 'approach' | 'encounter';
  /** Target celestial body identifier (e.g. `'planet'` for Primus or `'moon'` for Parvus). */
  readonly bodyId: string;
  /** Periapsis or low-side radius expressed as a multiple of `body.radius`. */
  readonly low: number;
  /** Apoapsis or high-side radius expressed as a multiple of `body.radius`. */
  readonly high: number;
  /** Optional starting apsis for elliptical placements (`'periapsis'` or `'apoapsis'`). */
  readonly at?: 'periapsis' | 'apoapsis';
}

Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons.

AcademyTarget §

Optional geometric teaching-guide ellipse ratios (`low` and `high` as multiples of `body.radius`). Purely illustrative: never predicts burns, steers the ship or alters simulation state.

export interface AcademyTarget {
  /** Periapsis radius of the suggested guide orbit as a multiple of `body.radius`. */
  readonly low: number;
  /** Apoapsis radius of the suggested guide orbit as a multiple of `body.radius`. */
  readonly high: number;
}

Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

OrbitalLesson §

export interface OrbitalLesson extends TutorialLesson {

  readonly practice: AcademyPractice;
  /** Optional suggested target orbit ratios (`low` and `high` in multiples of `body.radius`) drawn as a cyan teaching guide. */
  readonly target?: AcademyTarget;
}

AcademyFlight §

Detached committed flight state, SI and body-relative inertial coordinates. No mutable session/world reference crosses the learning boundary. Tutorials read this snapshot to derive `TutorialObservation` values and never write back to the world.

export interface AcademyFlight {
  /** Opaque continuity token for the controlled session, ship and teleport/respawn epoch. */
  readonly identity: object;
  /** Committed simulation time in seconds. */
  readonly time: number;
  /** Whether the flight session is currently paused. */
  readonly paused: boolean;
  /** Detached ship state in body-relative SI coordinates (metres, m/s, radians). */
  readonly state: State;
  /** Whether the ship's main engine is currently firing. */
  readonly engine: boolean;
  /** Active gravitating body parameters in SI units (`radius`, `surfaceTop` and `soi` in metres; `mu` in m³/s²). */
  readonly body: { readonly id: string; readonly name: string; readonly radius: number; readonly mu: number;
    readonly surfaceTop: number; readonly soi: number };
  /** Analytic two-body Keplerian elements when `analytic` is `true` and the ship is flying, or `null`. */
  readonly orbit: Elements | null;
  /** Height above terrain or sea level in metres, or `null` when unavailable. */
  readonly clearance: number | null;
  /** Whether the active motion model provides analytic two-body orbital elements. */
  readonly analytic: boolean;
  /** Current trajectory prediction end classification (e.g. `'closed'`, `'impact'`, `'escape'`). */
  readonly prediction: string;
  /** Count of valid planned maneuver nodes for the selected ship. */
  readonly plannedNodes: number;
  /** Total planned delta-v magnitude in m/s across valid maneuver nodes. */
  readonly plannedDeltaV: number;
  /** Count of pending (unhandled) orbital alarms. */
  readonly alarms: number;
}

Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

AcademyPanelOptions §

Host adapters supplied when mounting an `AcademyPanel`. Keeps the Academy UI decoupled from mutable sessions: `read()` returns detached snapshots and `practice()` runs only on explicit user click.

export interface AcademyPanelOptions {
  /** Borrowed viewport container element into which the HUD reopen button and floating window are mounted. */
  readonly container: HTMLElement;
  /** Window factory used to create the pinned Academy floating window (`id: 'academy'`). */
  readonly windows: Pick<WindowSystem, 'create'>;
  /** Window shell used to register the Academy dock instrument button. */
  readonly shell: Pick<WindowShell, 'addInstrument'>;
  /** Optional local storage backend for lesson completion progress. */
  readonly storage?: ThemePreferences;
  /** Learner/seat identifier appended to the course storage key (`<course.id>/v<course.version>/<learnerKey>`). */
  readonly learnerKey: string;
  /** Returns a detached `AcademyFlight` snapshot for the currently selected owned ship, or `undefined` when no owned ship is selected. */
  read(): AcademyFlight | undefined;
  /** Actual current bindings, resolved by the host; never hard-coded by lessons. */
  bindings(): string;
  /** Explicit button action only. The host checks local authority/ownership again. */
  practice(setup: AcademyPractice): void;
}

Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

AcademyPanel §

Mounted Orbital Academy UI panel and world-space target orbit overlay source. Disposing removes the HUD button, disposes the dock instrument, window and tutorial runner.

export interface AcademyPanel extends Disposable {
  /**
   * Advances UI and tutorial runner state by `elapsed` wall-clock seconds (throttled internally to 0.1 s intervals).
   * Reads `options.read()` and never mutates simulation state.
   */
  update(elapsed: number): void;
  /**
   * Returns world-space `OverlayPrimitive` line segments (in metres) for the suggested target orbit when a lesson with a target
   * is active, un-interrupted, checked by the learner and orbiting the viewed body; otherwise returns `[]`. Reuses preallocated line objects.
   */
  draw(view: PresentationView): readonly OverlayPrimitive[];
}

Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

AcademyPanelFactory §

Factory creating mounted `AcademyPanel` instances (`learning.academy@1.0.0`). Candidate-owned: disposing the provider disposes all active panels and rejects subsequent `create()` calls.

export interface AcademyPanelFactory {
  /** Mounts the Academy window, dock instrument, HUD objective button and tutorial runner. Throws if the provider is disposed. */
  create(options: AcademyPanelOptions): AcademyPanel;
}

Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

API reference index

game-api: universe.ts §

Declarations from universe.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/universe.ts.

SOI §

Sphere-of-influence radii in metres (`radius`, optional hysteresis `entryMetres` and `exitMetres`) for gravitational handoffs.

export interface SOI {
  readonly radius: number;
  readonly entryMetres?: number;
  readonly exitMetres?: number;
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

Ephemeris §

Prescribed celestial body trajectory relative to its parent body: fixed inertial coordinates (`m`, `m/s`) or circular Keplerian orbit (`m`, `rad`, `s`).

export type Ephemeris =
  | {
      readonly kind: 'fixed-inertial';
      readonly position?: readonly [number, number];
      readonly velocity?: readonly [number, number];
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'circular-kepler';
      readonly radius?: number;
      readonly radiusMetres?: number;
      readonly phaseRadiansAtEpoch?: number;
      readonly direction?: number;
      readonly period?: number;
    };

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

CelestialBodyDefinition §

Authored celestial body definition with SI physical parameters (`radius`/`radiusMetres` in `m`, `mass` in `kg`, `mu` in `m³/s²`), ephemeris, terrain, and appearance.

export interface CelestialBodyDefinition {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly parentId?: string | null;
  readonly radius?: number;
  readonly radiusMetres?: number;
  readonly mass?: number; // kg
  readonly mu?: number; // m^3 / s^2
  readonly soi?: SOI;
  readonly color?: string;
  readonly accentColor?: string;
  readonly atmosphereColor?: string;
  readonly horizonColor?: string;
  readonly skyColor?: string;
  readonly surfaceFeatures?: 'globe-lines' | 'craters' | 'smooth' | 'star';
  readonly surface?: SurfaceDefinition;
  readonly ephemeris?: Ephemeris;
  /** Sidereal spin; absent bodies do not rotate. */
  readonly rotation?: RotationDefinition;
  /** How the body is drawn; derived from `surfaceFeatures` and its colours when absent. */
  readonly appearance?: BodyAppearance;
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

AppearanceStyle §

Procedural look of a body. Presentation only: it never changes the physical outline.

export type AppearanceStyle = 'rocky' | 'arid' | 'oceanic' | 'banded' | 'icy' | 'hazy' | 'volcanic' | 'star';

Used by: spacelab.planet-designer, spacelab.render-webgl.

BodyAppearance §

Presentation-only procedural shader appearance parameters for a celestial body (style, `#rrggbb` palette, seed in `[0, 1000]`, and `[0, 1]` tuning sliders).

export interface BodyAppearance {
  readonly style: AppearanceStyle;
  /** Up to four '#rrggbb' colours. Their roles depend on the style:
   * rocky [ground, dark lava seas, bright highlands, polar frost], arid [sand, rock, salt flats, polar ice],
   * oceanic [ocean, vegetation, desert, ice and cloud],
   * banded [band, dark band, light band, storms], icy [ice, cracks, frost, tint], hazy [cloud, dark cloud, light cloud, tint],
   * volcanic [rock, dark crust, lava, sulfur], star [core, surface, limb, spots]. */
  readonly colors?: readonly string[];
  /** Pattern seed, 0 to 1000; derived from the body id when absent. */
  readonly seed?: number;
  /** Tuning from 0 to 1, 0.5 by default: sea level (or dry basins), cloud cover, polar ice,
   * crater density, turbulence of bands and clouds, and volcanic glow. */
  readonly water?: number;
  readonly clouds?: number;
  readonly ice?: number;
  readonly craters?: number;
  readonly turbulence?: number;
  readonly glow?: number;
}

Used by: spacelab.planet-designer.

AppearanceTuning §

Tunable appearance settings.

export type AppearanceTuning = 'water' | 'clouds' | 'ice' | 'craters' | 'turbulence' | 'glow';

Used by: spacelab.planet-designer.

APPEARANCE_TUNING §

Presentation tuning fields in shader order.

export const APPEARANCE_TUNING: readonly AppearanceTuning[] = Object.freeze(['water', 'clouds', 'ice', 'craters', 'turbulence', 'glow']);

Used by: spacelab.planet-designer.

RotationDefinition §

Authored sidereal rotation specification for a celestial body (`period` in `s`, `direction` `-1` CW or `1` CCW, `phaseRadiansAtEpoch` in `rad`).

export interface RotationDefinition {
  /** Seconds per turn. */
  readonly period: number;
  /** -1 (default) turns clockwise like default orbits, so a prograde launch gains the spin. */
  readonly direction?: number;
  readonly phaseRadiansAtEpoch?: number;
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

PresetDefinition §

Authored ship spawn preset definition relative to `bodyId` (`'landed'`, `'circular-orbit'`, or explicit body-relative SI `'state'`).

export interface PresetDefinition {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly bodyId: string;
  readonly kind: 'landed' | 'circular-orbit' | 'state';
  readonly radius?: number;
  readonly radiusMetres?: number;
  readonly altitude?: number;
  readonly phaseRadians?: number;
  readonly direction?: number;
  readonly headingRadians?: number;
  readonly zoom?: number;
  readonly position?: readonly [number, number];
  readonly velocity?: readonly [number, number];
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

VehicleDefaults §

Authoritative spacecraft handling parameters in SI units: full-thrust `acceleration` (`m/s²`), `turnRate` (`rad/s`), and `initialThrottle` (`[0, 1]`).

export interface VehicleDefaults {
  readonly acceleration: number;
  readonly turnRate: number;
  readonly initialThrottle: number;
  /** Optional circular hull radius in metres, [0, 32]; absent/zero retains legacy point contact.
   * The state position is the hull centre. Dry terrain and smooth bodies are supported;
   * finite hulls on ocean terrain are rejected rather than reinterpreted as point vessels. */
  readonly contactRadiusMetres?: number;
}

Used by: builtin.motion, spacelab.maneuver-burn.

SystemDefinition §

Authored star or planetary system definition containing hierarchical bodies, default vehicle handling, and spawn presets.

export interface SystemDefinition {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly description?: string;
  readonly centralBodyId?: string;
  readonly bodies: readonly CelestialBodyDefinition[];
  readonly vehicleDefaults?: Partial<VehicleDefaults>;
  readonly presets: readonly PresetDefinition[];
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

PhysicsMode §

Celestial body motion model: analytic Keplerian `'rails'` (default), numerical mutual-gravity `'nbody'`, or `'full-body'` (N-body plus optional J2, tides and collisions).

export type PhysicsMode = 'rails' | 'nbody' | 'full-body';

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

CollisionMode §

How touching celestial bodies interact in simulated universes: inelastic `'merge'`, impulse `'bounce'` (see `restitution`), or `'none'` (pass through).

export type CollisionMode = 'merge' | 'bounce' | 'none';

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

UniversePhysicsConfig §

Authored, optional universe physics settings. Omitted fields resolve to defaults (`resolveUniversePhysics` in `packages/sim-core`); an omitted or empty config means analytic rails, and is left out of the validated universe so legacy digests are unchanged. Settings other than `mode` and `maxWarp` are inert in `'rails'` mode.

export interface UniversePhysicsConfig {
  /** Body motion model; `'rails'` when absent. */
  readonly mode?: PhysicsMode;
  /** Integrator substeps per fixed simulation step, integer in `[1, 64]`. */
  readonly substeps?: number;
  /** Gravitational softening length as a fraction of the attracting body's radius (dimensionless, `>= 0`). */
  readonly softeningRatio?: number;
  /** Apply J2 oblateness of spinning bodies (`'full-body'` only). */
  readonly enableJ2?: boolean;
  /** Apply tidal spin dissipation (`'full-body'` only). */
  readonly enableTidalTorque?: boolean;
  /** Body–body contact response (`'full-body'` only). */
  readonly collisionMode?: CollisionMode;
  /** Coefficient of restitution for `'bounce'` collisions, in `[0, 1]`. */
  readonly restitution?: number;
  /** Highest permitted time-warp factor, in `[1, 100000]`. */
  readonly maxWarp?: number;
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

ResolvedUniversePhysics §

Universe physics settings with every default applied.

export type ResolvedUniversePhysics = Required<UniversePhysicsConfig>;

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

UniverseDefinition §

Portable multi-system universe definition (`schemaVersion`, metadata, `defaultSystemId`, `systems`, and optional `physics`).

export interface UniverseDefinition {
  readonly schemaVersion: number;
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly description?: string;
  readonly author?: string;
  readonly defaultSystemId: string;
  readonly systems: readonly SystemDefinition[];
  /** Body motion settings; absent means analytic Keplerian rails. */
  readonly physics?: UniversePhysicsConfig;
}

Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

ResolvedBody §

Fully validated celestial body with resolved SI physical constants (`radius` in `m`, `mass` in `kg`, `mu` in `m³/s²`), SOI hysteresis radii, surface identity, and 4-color appearance.

export interface ResolvedBody extends Body {
  readonly surfaceIdentity: string;
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly parentId: string | null;
  readonly radius: number;
  readonly mass: number;
  readonly mu: number;
  readonly soi: {
    readonly radius: number;
    readonly entryMetres: number;
    readonly exitMetres: number;
  };
  readonly color: string;
  readonly accentColor: string;
  readonly atmosphereColor?: string;
  readonly horizonColor?: string;
  readonly skyColor?: string;
  readonly surfaceFeatures: 'globe-lines' | 'craters' | 'smooth' | 'star';
  readonly ephemeris: Ephemeris;
  /** Always four colours once resolved. */
  readonly appearance: BodyAppearance & { readonly colors: readonly [string, string, string, string] };
}

Used by: spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.planet-designer.

LoadedSystem §

Resolved planetary system instance providing ephemeris queries (`getBodyPosition` in `m`, `getBodyVelocity` in `m/s` at simulation `time` in `s`), preset placement, and SOI transitions.

export interface LoadedSystem {
  readonly universeId: string;
  readonly universeName: string;
  readonly system: SystemDefinition;
  readonly rootBody: ResolvedBody;
  readonly vehicleDefaults: VehicleDefaults;
  readonly presets: readonly PresetDefinition[];
  getResolvedBody(id: string): ResolvedBody;
  getAllBodies(): readonly ResolvedBody[];
  getMoonsOf(parentId: string): readonly ResolvedBody[];
  getBodyPosition(bodyId: string, time: number): Vec;
  getBodyVelocity(bodyId: string, time: number): Vec;
  initialState(presetId?: string, surface?: SurfaceProfile): { state: State; body: ResolvedBody };
  checkSOI(state: State, currentBodyId: string, time: number): {
    switched: boolean;
    newBody: ResolvedBody;
    newState: State;
  };
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

game-api: universe-saving.ts §

Declarations from universe-saving.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/universe-saving.ts.

UniverseSaveFormat §

Host universe container package codec interface (`core.universe-save-format@1.0.0`). Encodes WorldSave structures into zip-compressed .spaceuniverse archives and decodes byte buffers. Decoding is inert and never executes saved module scripts or activates unapproved packages. Throws when archive format is unrecognised, corrupted, or when abort is signalled.

export interface UniverseSaveFormat {
  encode(save: WorldSave, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>;
  decode(bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<unknown>;
}

Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

CORE_UNIVERSE_FORMAT §

Service key for host universe container package codec (`core.universe-save-format@1.0.0`).

export const CORE_UNIVERSE_FORMAT: ServiceKey<UniverseSaveFormat> = Object.freeze({slot:'core.universe-save-format',version:'1.0.0'});

Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

UniverseSaveSource §

Snapshot of session and world identity captured prior to asynchronous save or load IO. Holds read-only world access, optional snapshot configuration, and permission flags.

export interface UniverseSaveSource {
  /** Stable session/world identity; captured before asynchronous work. */
  readonly identity: object;
  readonly world: WorldRead;
  readonly options?: SavedWorldOptions;
  readonly canSave: boolean;
  readonly canLoad: boolean;
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

UniverseSaveOptions §

Host callbacks and session access supplied when instantiating a UniverseSaveCommands controller. Defines current world resolution and atomic, guarded state restoration.

export interface UniverseSaveOptions {
  current(): UniverseSaveSource;
  /** Prepare exact installed packages, validate state, then atomically publish. Call
   * guard immediately before publication; observe signal during asynchronous work. */
  restore(raw: unknown, signal: AbortSignal, guard: () => void): Promise<void>;
}

Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

UniverseSaveCommands §

Single-operation universe save and load command controller (`universe.saves@1.0.0`). Serializes save/load actions, validates session consistency across async boundaries, and delegates payload encoding/decoding without activating untrusted scripts. Throws if another save/load is already in flight, if permissions are denied, or on session drift.

export interface UniverseSaveCommands extends Disposable {
  save(write: (name: string, bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<void>, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<WorldSave>;
  load(read: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
}

Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

UniverseSaveFactory §

Factory creating cancellable single-operation universe save and load command controllers (`universe.saves@1.0.0`).

export interface UniverseSaveFactory { create(options: UniverseSaveOptions): UniverseSaveCommands }

Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

UNIVERSE_SAVES §

Service key for the universe save/load command controller factory (`universe.saves@1.0.0`).

export const UNIVERSE_SAVES: ServiceKey<UniverseSaveFactory> = Object.freeze({slot:'universe.saves',version:'1.0.0'});

Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

UniverseFiles §

Bounded browser file dialog and download management service (`universe.files@1.0.0`). Reads user-selected Blobs up to 64 MiB and writes download files via temporary object URLs. Created URLs are automatically revoked after a 10-second timeout or immediately upon provider disposal. Throws if file sizes exceed 64 MiB, filenames contain invalid characters, or if aborted.

export interface UniverseFiles extends Disposable {
  read(file: Blob, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>;
  write(name: string, bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
}

Used by: spacelab.universe-files, spacelab.universe-save-controls.

UNIVERSE_FILES §

Service key for the browser universe file reader and writer (`universe.files@1.0.0`).

export const UNIVERSE_FILES: ServiceKey<UniverseFiles> = Object.freeze({slot:'universe.files',version:'1.0.0'});

Used by: spacelab.universe-files, spacelab.universe-save-controls.

UniverseDisk §

ServerUniverseOptions §

Configuration options for initializing a ServerUniverseStore instance. Defines target directory, backup retention count, state restore logic, and configuration error classification.

export interface ServerUniverseOptions<T> {
  readonly directory: string; readonly keepBackups: number;
  restore(raw: unknown): T | Promise<T>;
  /** Missing/incompatible packages require user recovery, not quarantine or fallback. */
  configurationError(error: unknown): boolean;
  log?(message: string): void;
}

Used by: spacelab.universe-server-storage.

ServerUniverseStore §

Persistent server universe storage managing world.json persistence, atomic writes, and backups. Quarantines unparseable files, falls back to timestamped backups, and preserves existing worlds on configuration error. Enqueues asynchronous writes in arrival order; idle() drains the queue prior to shutdown.

export interface ServerUniverseStore<T> extends Disposable {
  readonly file: string; readonly backups: string;
  load(): Promise<{readonly loaded: T; readonly from: string} | undefined>;
  save(save: WorldSave): Promise<void>;
  saveSync(save: WorldSave): void;
  idle(): Promise<void>;
  backup(): Promise<string | undefined>;
  backupFiles(): Promise<string[]>;
}

Used by: spacelab.universe-server-storage.

VehicleAppearance §

Vector artwork (+X forward), shared by Canvas and WebGL. Units are CSS pixels unless metresPerUnit opts into world-space scaling. The hull is a triangle fan and a closed outline. The flame tip extends with normalized throttle. This optional presentation data never changes authority or physical geometry.

export interface VehicleAppearance {
  /** Namespaced vehicle identifier matching a declared VehicleDefinition id. */
  readonly vehicleId: string;
  /** Hull vertices in local artwork units relative to the craft centre (3 to 128 points). */
  readonly hull: readonly Vec[];
  /** Optional world metres per artwork unit. Close views use physical scale; below
   * 8 CSS pixels across, renderers show a position marker. Omitted retains pixel art. */
  readonly metresPerUnit?: number;
  /** Optional open detail paths in the same local units, e.g. cockpit and hatch. */
  readonly lines?: readonly (readonly Vec[])[];
  /** Optional thruster exhaust flame dimensions for active acceleration visuals. */
  readonly flame?: { readonly x: number; readonly halfWidth: number; readonly length: number; readonly throttleLength: number };
}

Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.trainer-appearance.

game-api: view-hud.ts §

Declarations from view-hud.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/view-hud.ts.

HudBinding §

Per-view binding tying a HUD view to a seat, viewport, session, and optional context.

export interface HudBinding extends LocalViewBinding { readonly session: object; readonly context?: object }

Used by: spacelab.player-view, spacelab.view-hud.

HudSurface §

The pane an instrument draws in. Sizes and offsets are CSS pixels; `root` is the DOM parent the instrument may append its own elements to and must remove them on dispose.

export interface HudSurface { readonly root: HTMLElement; readonly width: number; readonly height: number; readonly x: number; readonly y: number }

Used by: spacelab.throttle-hud, spacelab.view-hud.

HudAdapter §

Service adapter injected into a HUD view for instrument access.

export interface HudAdapter<T = unknown> { readonly key: Pick<ServiceKey<T>, 'slot' | 'version'>; readonly service: T }

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

HudInstrumentContext §

Per-instrument initialization context providing access to declared required adapters.

export interface HudInstrumentContext {
  readonly binding: HudBinding;
  /** Returns an adapter listed in the definition's `requires`; throws for any other key. */
  use<T>(key: ServiceKey<T>): T;
}

Used by: builtin.navball.

HudInstrument §

One instrument instance for one seat and viewport, owned by a ViewHud. A method that throws disables only this instrument: the owner cancels and disposes it and reports the fault.

export interface HudInstrument extends Disposable {
  /** Stop any pointer or keyboard gesture in progress; a new gesture is needed afterwards. */
  cancel(): void;
  /** The seat's session, player or target changed. Gestures were cancelled first. */
  rebind(binding: HudBinding): void;
  resize(surface: HudSurface): void;
  /** Once per rendered frame. `elapsed` is wall-clock seconds since the previous frame,
   * not simulation time; do not advance or write the world here. */
  update(elapsed: number): void;
  /** Append overlay shapes for this frame; shapes appended before a throw are discarded. */
  draw(into: OverlayPrimitive[]): void;
}

Used by: builtin.navball, spacelab.view-hud.

HudInstrumentDefinition §

A selectable instrument, published as a service such as `THROTTLE_HUD`. Stateless and reusable: the HUD owner calls `create` once per seat and disposes each instance.

export interface HudInstrumentDefinition {
  /** Namespaced ID, such as `spacelab.throttle-hud`; unique within one selection. */
  readonly id: string;
  /** Exact-version, read-only view adapters. An incompatible view omits this contribution. */
  readonly requires: readonly Pick<ServiceKey<unknown>, 'slot' | 'version'>[];
  create(context: HudInstrumentContext): HudInstrument;
}

Used by: builtin.navball, spacelab.throttle-hud, spacelab.view-hud.

ViewHud §

Per-seat HUD controller coordinating active instruments, surface sizing, updates and overlays.

export interface ViewHud extends Disposable {
  readonly binding: HudBinding;
  readonly count: number;
  bind(binding: HudBinding): void;
  select(instruments: readonly HudInstrumentDefinition[]): void;
  /** Cancel instrument gestures without removing the current selection. */
  cancel?(): void;
  resize(surface: HudSurface): void;
  update(elapsed: number): void;
  draw(into: OverlayPrimitive[]): void;
}

Used by: spacelab.view-hud.

ViewHudOptions §

Configuration options for instantiating a ViewHud.

export interface ViewHudOptions {
  readonly binding: HudBinding;
  readonly adapters: readonly HudAdapter[];
  readonly instruments?: readonly HudInstrumentDefinition[];
  fault?(id: string, error: unknown): void;
}

Used by: spacelab.view-hud.

ViewHudFactory §

Creates one ViewHud per seat and viewport. Each ViewHud owns the instruments it creates.

export interface ViewHudFactory extends Disposable { create(options: ViewHudOptions): ViewHud }

Used by: spacelab.view-hud.

VIEW_HUD §

Service key for the shared per-view HUD factory (`ui.view-hud@1.0.0`).

export const VIEW_HUD: ServiceKey<ViewHudFactory> = Object.freeze({slot:'ui.view-hud',version:'1.0.0'});

Used by: spacelab.view-hud.

ThrottleLeverState §

Throttle readout for one seat. `throttle` is a fraction from 0 to 1; Space Sim reports 0 while the engine is not firing. `locked` means the host is ignoring throttle input, for example while paused. `world` and `shipId` identify the target so a gesture can detect a switch.

export interface ThrottleLeverState {
  readonly world: object; readonly shipId: string; readonly throttle: number; readonly locked: boolean; readonly firing: boolean;
}

Used by: spacelab.throttle-hud.

ThrottleLeverOptions §

Keyboard increments as fractions of full thrust in (0, 1]. Defaults: 0.01 and 0.1.

export interface ThrottleLeverOptions { readonly keyboardStep?: number; readonly pageStep?: number }

Used by: spacelab.throttle-hud.

FlightHudReadout §

Detached/read-only flight readout in the body's inertial frame. No world-step or write API.

export interface FlightHudReadout {
  readonly state: State; readonly radius: number; readonly spin: number; readonly clearance: number | null;
  readonly center: Vec; readonly scale: number; readonly cameraRotation: number;
  readonly frameSetting: number; readonly orientation: 'horizon' | 'camera';
  readonly accentColor?: string; readonly playerLabel?: string; readonly trajectory?: string;
  readonly pinned: boolean; readonly dragging: boolean; readonly editing: boolean;
  readonly maneuver?: number;
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

FlightHudAdapter §

Per-seat flight bridge supplied by the host as the `view.flight-hud-state` adapter. Reads return detached values or undefined when the seat has no flyable target.

export interface FlightHudAdapter {
  /** Zero-based local seat index; Player 1 is 0. */
  readonly pilot: number;
  navball(): FlightHudReadout | undefined;
  throttle(): ThrottleLeverState | undefined;
  /** Fit the independent lever's preferred pane-local position around host HUD obstacles.
   * Both input and result are CSS pixels; callers must not retain the scratch input. */
  placeThrottle?(preferred: Vec, surface: HudSurface): Vec;
  /** Request thrust as a fraction from 0 to 1. The adapter rechecks session ownership, target
   * and input suppression at command time and ignores out-of-range or locked requests. */
  setThrottle(value: number): void;
  cancelGestures?(): void;
}

Used by: builtin.navball.

FLIGHT_HUD_STATE §

Host adapter for flight instruments. Declare it in `HudInstrumentDefinition.requires`.

export const FLIGHT_HUD_STATE: ServiceKey<FlightHudAdapter> = Object.freeze({slot:'view.flight-hud-state',version:'1.0.0'});

Used by: builtin.navball, spacelab.throttle-hud.

game-api: webrtc-transport.ts §

Declarations from webrtc-transport.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/webrtc-transport.ts.

ConnectionDiagnostics §

Diagnostic metrics for a WebRTC peer connection. Exposes counts and state codes only; never IP addresses, raw SDP or secrets.

export interface ConnectionDiagnostics {
  /** Current ICE gathering state (e.g. 'new', 'gathering', 'complete'). */
  readonly gathering: RTCIceGatheringState;
  /** Current peer connection state (e.g. 'new', 'connecting', 'connected', 'disconnected', 'failed', 'closed'). */
  readonly connection: RTCPeerConnectionState;
  /** Count of gathered ICE candidates grouped by candidate type (host, srflx, relay). */
  readonly candidates: Readonly<Record<string, number>>;
  /** Numerical error codes encountered during ICE or handshake. */
  readonly errors: readonly number[];
}

Used by: spacelab.net-webrtc.

ManualInvite §

Active manual WebRTC invitation created by a session host.

export interface ManualInvite extends Disposable {
  /** Compact base64url encoded invitation code containing compressed SDP offer. */
  readonly code: string;
  /** Whether STUN reflex candidate gathering was enabled for this invitation. */
  readonly stun: boolean;
  /** Return current connection and ICE gathering diagnostics. */
  diagnostics(): ConnectionDiagnostics;
  /** Accept a guest's reply code to finalize the connection. Resolves to the established TextConnection. */
  accept(reply: string): Promise<TextConnection>;
}

Used by: spacelab.net-webrtc.

ManualReply §

Active manual WebRTC reply generated by a joining guest in response to an invitation.

export interface ManualReply extends Disposable {
  /** Compact base64url encoded reply code containing compressed SDP answer. */
  readonly code: string;
  /** Expiration timestamp in milliseconds (epoch) after which the reply is invalid. */
  readonly expiresAt: number;
  /** Return current connection and ICE gathering diagnostics. */
  diagnostics(): ConnectionDiagnostics;
  /** Resolves to the established TextConnection once the host accepts the reply. */
  readonly connection: Promise<TextConnection>;
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

ManualSignalingProvider §

Manual WebRTC signaling provider exchanging copy-paste invite/reply codes for peer-to-peer data channels.

export interface ManualSignalingProvider {
  /** Create a new invitation offer. Signal owns the invite lifecycle and pending gathering. */
  create(stun: boolean, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<ManualInvite>;
  /** Answer an invitation offer code, generating a reply code and pending connection promise. */
  answer(offer: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<ManualReply>;
  /** Validate whether text matches the expected invitation code format. */
  isInviteCode(text: string): boolean;
  /** Validate whether text matches the expected reply code format. */
  isReplyCode(text: string): boolean;
}

Used by: spacelab.net-webrtc.

WEBRTC_SIGNALING §

ServiceKey for manual WebRTC invite/reply signaling (slot `net.webrtc-signaling`).

export const WEBRTC_SIGNALING: ServiceKey<ManualSignalingProvider> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'net.webrtc-signaling', version: '1.0.0' });

Used by: spacelab.net-webrtc.

DocumentBlock §

Inert structured text block for portable window documents and guides. Links navigate the host's document catalog, never external URLs.

export type DocumentBlock =
  | { readonly type: 'paragraph' | 'heading' | 'code'; readonly text: string }
  | { readonly type: 'list'; readonly items: readonly string[]; readonly ordered?: boolean }
  | { readonly type: 'link'; readonly text: string; readonly page: string };

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

WindowDocument §

Validated immutable structured document composed of up to 256 document blocks.

export interface WindowDocument {
  readonly blocks: readonly DocumentBlock[];
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

PreviewData §

Bounded JSON-compatible value used as candidate data for registered window previews. Subject to recursive depth (max 16) and total element (max 4096) boundaries.

export type PreviewData = null | boolean | number | string | readonly PreviewData[] | { readonly [key: string]: PreviewData };

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

PreviewType §

Named, exact-version identifier descriptor for a registered preview component.

export interface PreviewType {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly version: string;
}

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

WindowContentDefinition §

Tagged union of content sections mountable inside window bodies or dialog layouts. Supports portable documents, interactive inspector forms, scrollable lists/logs, and custom previews.

export type WindowContentDefinition =
  | { readonly kind: 'document' | 'instruction'; readonly id: string; readonly document: WindowDocument }
  | { readonly kind: 'inspector' | 'form'; readonly id: string; readonly controls: readonly UiControl[] }
  | { readonly kind: 'list' | 'log'; readonly id: string; readonly items: readonly string[] }
  | { readonly kind: 'preview'; readonly id: string; readonly type: PreviewType; readonly data: PreviewData };

Used by: spacelab.ui-windows.

WindowContentOptions §

WindowPreviewType §

Extension descriptor for registering a custom versioned preview component (`ui.window-content@1.0.0`). Enforces data validation prior to resource allocation; never receives simulation state or world authority.

export interface WindowPreviewType {
  readonly type: PreviewType;
  /** Validate/capture data before creating resources. No authority is passed to a preview. */

  mount(root: HTMLElement, data: PreviewData, options: WindowContentOptions): WindowPreview;
}

Used by: spacelab.ui-windows.

WindowContentService §

Service for registering preview types and mounting structured content sections (`ui.window-content@1.0.0`). Disposing the service unregisters active preview types and cleans up mounted sections.

export interface WindowContentService extends Disposable {
  /** Owner-published named, exact-version extension; add-ons declare this service need. */
  register(preview: WindowPreviewType): Disposable;
  mount(root: HTMLElement, content: WindowContentDefinition, options?: WindowContentOptions): Disposable;
  document(root: HTMLElement, document: WindowDocument, options?: WindowContentOptions): Disposable;
}

Used by: spacelab.manual, spacelab.ui-windows.

WINDOW_CONTENT §

Service key for the window content mounting and preview registry service (`ui.window-content@1.0.0`).

Used by: spacelab.manual, spacelab.ui-windows.

captureDocument §

Headless parser and validator for portable guide and manual documents. Validates block structure, restricts character limits (<= 64 KiB total), and returns a deeply frozen document. Throws when block count exceeds 256, when text length exceeds 64 KiB, or when unsupported block fields exist.

export function captureDocument(value: unknown): WindowDocument;

Used by: spacelab.manual, spacelab.ui-windows.

documentText §

Extracts searchable raw plain text from a structured window document. Concatenates all paragraph, heading, code, and list item text separated by spaces.

export function documentText(document: WindowDocument): string;

Used by: spacelab.manual.

capturePreviewData §

Validates and deep-freezes candidate data for a window preview component. Recursively inspects data structure ensuring depth <= 16, total elements <= 4096, and encoded UTF-8 text <= 64 KiB. Throws when data exceeds structural or byte-size boundaries.

export function capturePreviewData(value: PreviewData): PreviewData;

Used by: spacelab.ui-windows.

WindowShellOptions §

Configuration options for creating a workspace window shell and dock (`ui.shell@1.0.0`). Defines the main viewport element, optional overlay root, grouped panel definitions, instrument toggle integration, wall-millisecond Escape debounce interval, chrome settings, and input focus/release lifecycle hooks.

export interface WindowShellOptions {
  readonly viewport: HTMLElement;
  readonly overlayRoot?: HTMLElement;

  focus(): void;
  onClose?(): void;

  dismissModal?(): boolean;
  filePanel?(input: HTMLInputElement): string | undefined;
}

Used by: spacelab.ui-shell.

WindowShell §

Workspace window shell managing an edge dock and floating window lifecycles (`ui.shell@1.0.0`). Unpinned windows behave like tabs (one at a time, closed by returning to flight or pressing Escape), while pinned windows stay open during flight. Manages Escape debounce, fullscreen exit detection, file-input change routing to workspace panels, and borrowed content node restoration on disposal. Throws when invoked after disposal or when opening an unregistered panel.

export interface WindowShell extends Disposable {
  readonly isOpen: boolean;
  addInstrument(window: WindowChrome, label: string, hint: string): Disposable;
  open(panel?: string): void;
  close(focus?: boolean, fromEscape?: boolean): void;
  toggle(fromEscape?: boolean): void;
  handleFullscreenEscape(): void;
}

Used by: spacelab.ui-shell.

WindowShellFactory §

Factory service creating workspace window shells (`ui.shell@1.0.0`). Injects shared shell stylesheet rules into target documents. Disposing the factory disposes all active shell instances and removes injected stylesheet elements.

export interface WindowShellFactory extends Disposable {
  create(options: WindowShellOptions): WindowShell;
}

Used by: spacelab.ui-shell.

WINDOW_SHELL §

Service key for the workspace window shell factory (`ui.shell@1.0.0`).

export const WINDOW_SHELL: ServiceKey<WindowShellFactory> = Object.freeze({slot:'ui.shell',version:'1.0.0'});

One independently controlled vessel. `state` is relative to `bodyId`'s non-rotating frame.

}

WorldEvent §

Authoritative event emitted during a fixed world step: surface contact, SOI transition, celestial body collision, per-ship motion fault, world rule fault, or module domain event.

export type WorldEvent =
  | { readonly type: 'world-fault'; readonly message: string }
  | { readonly type: 'contact'; readonly shipId: string; readonly contact: SurfaceContactEvent; readonly status: State['status'] }
  | { readonly type: 'soi'; readonly shipId: string; readonly from: string; readonly to: string }
  | { readonly type: 'fault'; readonly shipId: string; readonly message: string }
  | { readonly type: 'module'; readonly shipId: string; readonly moduleEvent: { readonly type: string; readonly payload: ModuleValue }; readonly event: { readonly type: string; readonly payload: ModuleValue } }
  | { readonly type: 'avatar-contact'; readonly avatarId: string; readonly bodyId: string; readonly fraction: number }
  | { readonly type: 'avatar-board'; readonly avatarId: string; readonly shipId: string }
  | { readonly type: 'avatar-exit'; readonly avatarId: string; readonly shipId: string }
  | {
      readonly type: 'body-collision';
      readonly collisionType: 'merge' | 'bounce';
      readonly survivorId?: string;
      readonly absorbedId?: string;
      readonly bodyAId?: string;
      readonly bodyBId?: string;
      readonly newMass?: number;
      readonly newRadius?: number;
      readonly newMu?: number;
      readonly impulse?: number;
      readonly time: number;
    };

Used by: spacelab.audio-controls, spacelab.session-flight.

API reference index

Academy flight adapter §

Explicit practice actions and read-only orbital guidance.

What it does

Provides the Orbital Academy flight window, dock instrument, HUD objective reopen button, SVG orbit comparison diagram and world-space target orbit overlay. The interface is a read-only observer during flight: it polls detached committed flight state through host-supplied callbacks, evaluates lesson steps through the tutorial runner, and never imports private host modules, runs timers, steers the ship or steps the simulation. Practice setups are invoked only when the learner explicitly clicks Load practice setup or Restart practice, where the host validates local ship ownership, cancels armed maneuvers, cuts thrust and pauses.

Provides and requires

  • Provides learning.academy@1.0.0 through the ACADEMY_PANELS service key: creates mounted AcademyPanel instances.
  • Fills the learning.academy slot in platform profiles.
  • Requires learning.runner@^1.0.0 through the TUTORIAL_RUNNERS service key: creates the TutorialRunner state machine that checks step conditions, holds and questions. Profile resolution fails if no learning.runner provider is enabled.
  • Requires learning.orbital-course@^1.0.0 through the ORBITAL_COURSE service key: supplies the lesson catalogue, practice setups and target orbit ratios. Profile resolution fails if no learning.orbital-course provider is enabled.

Settings

This module has no settings.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization resolves TUTORIAL_RUNNERS and ORBITAL_COURSE, reads assets/academy.css, registers a candidate-owned ACADEMY_PANELS factory and contributes this guide.
  • create(options) creates a TutorialRunner scoped to <course.id>/v<course.version>/<learnerKey>, builds the Academy DOM, opens a pinned window (id: 'academy'), appends the HUD button to options.container and registers the Academy dock instrument on options.shell. If dock registration throws, the HUD button, runner and window are cleaned up before rethrowing.
  • Starting a lesson checks that an owned ship is selected and analytic two-body orbital elements are active; practice buttons call options.practice(selected.practice) before capturing the initial observation and continuity identity.
  • update(seconds) throttles DOM and runner updates to 0.1 s wall-clock intervals while the window is open or a lesson is active, stops guidance if no owned ship is selected, and clears the target preview if the flight identity changes or the runner is interrupted.
  • draw(view) transforms the 97-point geometric target ellipse into world-space line segments around view.body.position without per-frame allocation when a target orbit is active and checked.
  • dispose() removes the HUD button and disposes the runner, dock entry and window; disposing the provider disposes all active panels.

Remove or replace

To customize the Academy layout, diagram or overlay styling while keeping the same curriculum and runner, provide another learning.academy@1.0.0 implementation in the learning.academy slot. Removing this module from a profile that does not require learning.academy removes the Academy window, dock button and target orbit overlays while leaving flight simulation and the Manual untouched.

Stability

Module version 0.1.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze: panel adapter callbacks and preview helpers may evolve before final freeze. Progress keys are namespaced as <course.id>/v<course.version>/<learnerKey>.

Example

examples/modules/academy-ui mounts an Academy panel against minimal window, shell and flight adapters, starts guidance and draws the target orbit overlay. Build and validate it from the repository root:

npm run module:build -- examples/modules/academy-ui/module.json /tmp/example-academy-ui.spacemod
npm run module:validate -- /tmp/example-academy-ui.spacemod

Alarms dock instrument §

Optional read-only window over independent orbital clocks.

What it does

This module contributes a dock instrument button and floating inspection window for viewing pending and delivered orbital alarms. It reads active simulation-time events from the flight alarm catalogue, formats relative countdowns and event descriptions, and presents polite live-region updates without scheduling, modifying, or executing flight commands.

Provides and requires

  • Provides view.alarms@1.0.0 through the ALARM_PANELS service key: creates AlarmPanel UI controller instances.
  • Fills the view.alarms slot in platform profiles.
  • Requires flight.alarm-catalogue@^1.0.0: queries active reminders and delivered status.
  • Requires ui.windows@^1.0.0 (optional): creates floating chrome windows in supported window managers.

Settings

This module has no settings.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization resolves the alarm catalogue and optional window manager, registering ALARM_PANELS.
  • create(options) mounts an Alarms window, adds the clock instrument to the dock shell, and binds change listeners.
  • update() queries the catalogue at a throttled rate (minimum 200 ms between UI passes) to update countdowns and status text.
  • dispose unregisters the dock instrument and closes the window chrome.

Remove or replace

To customize alarm listing presentation or integrate with an alternative windowing framework, contribute an alternative provider for view.alarms@1.0.0 to the view.alarms slot. Removing this module removes the dock button and panel while underlying alarm clocks continue operating.

Stability

Module version 0.1.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze: DOM container structures and throttle interval timings may evolve prior to final API freeze.

Vehicle vector artwork §

Shared optional Canvas/WebGL hull and flame data.

What it does

This module contributes the standard vector appearance for the orbital trainer ship (spacelab.trainer/orbit-trainer). It defines screen-space hull geometry (+X forward chevron outline with four vertices) and thruster flame geometry for Canvas 2D and WebGL 2 renderers. The artwork is purely cosmetic presentation data and never alters vessel mass, propulsion, collision bounds, flight mechanics or saved games.

Provides and requires

  • The module provides no service keys, requiring no runtime dependencies.
  • Contributes a VehicleAppearance definition via context.contributeVehicleAppearance for vehicle ID spacelab.trainer/orbit-trainer.
  • Contributes this usage guide for creators and the flight guide for players.

Settings

surveyLander (default 0, from 0 to 1, step 1): 0 keeps the Classic pixel chevron; 1 selects the physical survey lander artwork. See the physical artwork notes below.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization contributes trainerAppearance to the module context and registers creator and player documentation guides.
  • The module owns no persistent runtime state or active timers.
  • Renderers retrieve vehicle appearance via createVehicleArtwork and draw the chevron hull and throttle flame during frame rendering.

Remove or replace

Removing or replacing this module causes renderers to fall back to the generic four-point box marker (VEHICLE_MARKER) when drawing the trainer. Handling and flight characteristics remain unaffected. To provide alternative vehicle artwork, implement a presentation module calling contributeVehicleAppearance with custom geometry.

Stability

Module version 0.1.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze. Vehicle ID is spacelab.trainer/orbit-trainer.

Example

Optional surveyLander=1 selects a 5 m circular lander with SI geometry (metresPerUnit=1), cockpit and hatch detail paths; pair it with authoritative contactRadiusMetres=2.5. Default 0 preserves Classic artwork. World-space artwork becomes a generic navigation marker below eight projected CSS pixels; it never changes physics.

The survey lander uses original code-drawn vectors: a glazed cabin with a static seated suit silhouette, a recessed expanding main-engine bell, small attitude-control ports and a 2 m side hatch. All geometry remains within its 2.5 m circular protective hull. This is appearance only; the successor prototype uses existing unlimited propulsion and has no crew/interior gameplay yet.

Flight sound controls §

Public audio.flight-controls@1.0.0 service and lifecycle.

What it does

Bridges browser sound settings UI, user-gesture activation, document visibility and local preference storage to an optional AudioProvider. Flight code supplies read-only observations (thrust, pause, time warp, faults and terrain or smooth-body contacts); this module never mutates simulation state. Confirmed terrain contacts and smooth-body status transitions play at most one land, crash or splash cue per increasing simulation timestamp, and continuous thrust is silenced during pause, time warp above 1x, faults, hidden tabs or inactive views.

Provides and requires

  • Provides audio.flight-controls@1.0.0 through the FLIGHT_SOUND service key: creates or mounts FlightAudioControl instances.
  • Fills the audio.flight-controls slot in platform profiles.
  • Requires nothing. An AudioProvider (such as audio.procedural@1.0.0) is attached dynamically via control.setProvider(provider); when no provider is set, controls remain usable and display instructions to enable Procedural sound.

Settings

  • volume (default 0.4, from 0 to 1, step 0.01): initial linear gain for viewers without saved audio preferences, where 0 is silent and 1 is full gain; browser gesture activation is still required.
  • enabled (default 1, from 0 to 1, step 1): 0 starts sound disabled and 1 enables sound after a permitted browser gesture; a saved viewer preference in storage takes precedence.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization reads volume and enabled settings, registers a candidate-owned FLIGHT_SOUND factory and contributes this guide.
  • create(options) binds to borrowed toggle, slider and status elements in a browser document (throwing if defaultVolume is outside [0, 1] or ownerDocument.defaultView is absent) and loads saved preferences from storage key space-sim.audio/1.
  • mount(options) validates prefix against /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$/, replaces options.root children with the scoped sound settings template, and restores the original child nodes when the returned control is disposed or if creation throws.
  • Trusted pointerdown or keydown gestures, toggle clicks and slider inputs activate and resume the bound AudioOutput; visibilitychange and pagehide suspend output when hidden.
  • reset(contactTime) zeroes thrust and sets the simulation-time contact watermark so world loads or session reconnects do not replay old contacts.
  • Disposing a control aborts DOM listeners, disposes its AudioOutput and restores any mounted root children; disposing the provider disposes all active controls.

Remove or replace

Provide another audio.flight-controls@1.0.0 implementation in the audio.flight-controls slot to customize sound settings UI or cue routing. If this module is removed, the host hides the sound settings section and substitutes a silent no-op controller while gameplay and rendering continue normally.

Stability

Module version 0.1.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze: control options and status messages may change before final freeze. Viewer preferences persist under storage key space-sim.audio/1.

Example

examples/modules/audio-controls mounts flight sound controls into a container, attaches a custom AudioProvider and routes thrust and contact cues. Build and validate it from the repository root:

npm run module:build -- examples/modules/audio-controls/module.json /tmp/example-audio-controls.spacemod
npm run module:validate -- /tmp/example-audio-controls.spacemod

Procedural sound §

Public audio.procedural@1.0.0 service and lifecycle.

What it does

Provides the sample-free Web Audio procedural synthesizer (builtin.audio) for engine thrust rumble and one-shot flight cues (confirm, pause, resume, warp, land, crash and splash). Synthesis is viewer-local and never feeds simulation state. Creating the provider or an AudioOutput is completely silent and allocates no AudioContext until resume() is called after a browser gesture. Engine noise and oscillator nodes are created once per output and reused; transient contact and notification voices are rate-limited to 0.06 s and capped at 8 concurrent voices.

Provides and requires

  • Provides audio.procedural@1.0.0 through the PROCEDURAL_SOUND service key: exposes the disposable AudioSynth provider.
  • Fills the audio.procedural slot in presentation profiles.
  • Requires nothing. Host sound controls (such as spacelab.audio-controls) receive the provider when enabled and remain functional without audio when it is absent.

Settings

This module has no settings.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization registers a candidate-owned AudioSynth under PROCEDURAL_SOUND and contributes this guide without creating an AudioContext.
  • create() returns an isolated AudioOutput whose initial volume (default 0.4) and thrust (default 0) are stored in memory until audio starts; throws if the provider is disposed.
  • resume() lazily constructs the Web Audio graph (deterministic 2-second xorshift noise buffer, highpass/lowpass filters and sine sub-rumble oscillator) on first call and resumes the AudioContext.
  • setVolume(level) and setThrust(level) clamp non-finite or out-of-range inputs to [0, 1] and smoothly ramp Web Audio gain and filter targets; play(cue) schedules a short filtered noise or sine envelope when the context is running.
  • suspend() zeroes thrust, stops active transient voices and suspends the AudioContext.
  • Disposing an output stops and disconnects all continuous and transient nodes and closes its AudioContext; disposing the provider disposes every tracked output.

Remove or replace

Provide another audio.procedural@1.0.0 implementation in the audio.procedural slot to replace the synthesizer with custom procedural recipes or sample playback. Removing or disabling builtin.audio in ModMenu immediately disposes active outputs and leaves the simulation and renderer unaffected.

Stability

Module version 0.1.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2, retaining the builtin.audio module ID for profile compatibility. Both are pre-freeze: synthesis parameters and voice limits may be tuned before final freeze.

Example

examples/modules/audio-procedural wraps audio.procedural to create a managed sound output, clamp telemetry levels and trigger flight cues safely in headless or browser environments. Build and validate it from the repository root:

npm run module:build -- examples/modules/audio-procedural/module.json /tmp/example-audio-procedural.spacemod
npm run module:validate -- /tmp/example-audio-procedural.spacemod

Related

Code modules: manifests, packaging and installation (in-game Manual: modules)

Independent follow cameras §

Reusable basic camera control with an explicit flight adapter and optional smart-camera policy.

What it does

This module provides independent follow and free camera controllers across local viewports. It tracks target positions and orientations in double-precision world coordinates, projects celestial bodies and vehicles to viewport CSS pixels, and manages smooth zoom, panning, inertial fling gestures, and horizon alignment without altering physical simulation stepping.

Provides and requires

  • Provides view.camera@1.0.0 through the VIEW_CAMERAS service key: creates LocalViewCamera instances for multi-view and presentation compositions.
  • Provides view.flight-camera@1.0.0 through the FLIGHT_CAMERAS service key: provides the CameraController flight adapter supporting planet views, body-relative tracking, drag gestures, and rebasing.
  • Requires view.smart-camera@1.0.0 (optional) through the SMART_CAMERAS service key: enables candidate search, guided arrival transitions, and focus acquisition when available.
  • Fills the view.camera and view.flight-camera slots.

Settings

  • initialZoom (default 10, from 0.1 to 50): Starting magnification for each independent view. Changing a view later affects that view only.
  • referenceMetres (default 1500, from 1 to 1000000000): World metres framed by the shorter viewport dimension at zoom 1. Changes presentation scale only.
  • orientationSeconds (default 0.12, from 0 to 2, step 0.01): Wall seconds for camera rotation to approach the target orientation. Zero snaps immediately; simulation time is unchanged.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization captures initialZoom, referenceMetres, and orientationSeconds settings, queries optional SMART_CAMERAS, and contributes this guide.
  • Selected slots register follow cameras for VIEW_CAMERAS and flight cameras for FLIGHT_CAMERAS.
  • Each camera instance tracks zoom, pan velocity, orientation, and focus state, reprojecting frames during viewport reflow and rendering passes.
  • Disposing a camera releases fling state and focus bindings; disposing the provider terminates all active camera instances.

Remove or replace

To replace camera behavior, provide alternative services implementing view.camera@1.0.0 or view.flight-camera@1.0.0 in their respective slots. Removing this module without an alternative camera provider leaves viewports and flight splits without projection controllers.

Stability

Module version 0.3.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze: camera projection parameters, flight control hooks, and smart camera integration points may evolve before final API freeze.

Example

examples/modules/camera-follow demonstrates instantiating a flight camera, adjusting magnification, and tracking target coordinates. Build and validate it from the repository root:

npm run module:build -- examples/modules/camera-follow/module.json /tmp/example-camera-follow.spacemod
npm run module:validate -- /tmp/example-camera-follow.spacemod

Planet view derives its framing from referenceMetres: zoom is 0.35*referenceMetres/bodyRadius. Both the legacy 1500 m span and close-up spans therefore frame a full planet consistently. Choose referenceMetres=750 and initialZoom=30 for a human-scale landing view; scroll can zoom out to orbit.

Smart camera add-on §

Optional target search, guided arrival and focus tracking for compatible cameras.

What it does

This module provides optional targeting heuristics, guided arrival interpolations, and target tracking for compatible cameras. When a pilot flings or drags toward a cluster of vehicles or celestial bodies, the smart camera selects the most relevant candidate based on distance, screen velocity, and group priority, easing the view smoothly toward the acquired target.

Provides and requires

  • Provides view.smart-camera@1.0.0 through the SMART_CAMERAS service key: creates SmartCameraControl instances and candidate target selection functions.
  • Fills the view.smart-camera slot in presentation configurations.
  • Requires no external services.

Settings

This module has no settings.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization contributes this guide and registers the SMART_CAMERAS factory.
  • create() produces independent SmartCameraControl instances per viewport.
  • select(search) evaluates candidate target groups in prioritized order and returns the closest candidate matching trajectory vectors.
  • start(target, followOnArrival) initiates guided camera movement toward the target's position until arrival, optionally handing off to follow mode.
  • Disposing a control releases active tracking state; disposing the provider terminates all active smart camera controllers.

Remove or replace

This module is completely optional. If removed or omitted from a profile, camera systems fall back to standard follow and drag behaviors without target snapping or guided arrival curves.

Stability

Module version 0.1.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze: candidate target scoring and guidance interfaces may be refined prior to API freeze.

Example

examples/modules/camera-smart demonstrates selecting candidate targets and guiding a smart camera controller. Build and validate it from the repository root:

npm run module:build -- examples/modules/camera-smart/module.json /tmp/example-camera-smart.spacemod
npm run module:validate -- /tmp/example-camera-smart.spacemod

Chat console §

Optional session chat and command UI with owned DOM lifecycle.

What it does

This module renders the in-game text chat overlay and command entry console. It mounts an interactive UI into a caller-supplied container element, manages message history pruning up to a configured line count, supports keyboard shortcuts (Enter to focus, Slash to begin commands, Escape to minimize), displays message categories (system, error, success, player chat), and saves minimize state to local browser storage.

Provides and requires

  • Provides ui.chat@1.0.0 through the CHAT_CONSOLES key: a ChatConsoleFactory instance.
  • Requires no services at module initialization.

Settings

  • historyLines (default 60, from 1 to 1000, step 1): Maximum messages retained in this local chat window. This does not change server chat history, permissions or message limits.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization provides the ChatConsoleFactory and registers it for owned disposal.
  • create(options) mounts the chat HTML template and stylesheet into options.root, wiring keyboard listeners via AbortController.
  • log(kind, text, sender) and show(line, playerId) format and append message rows, auto-scrolling to the bottom and pruning old lines.
  • minimize(minimized, focusInput) toggles between full overlay and minimized pill views, persisting preference to space-sim:chat:minimized.
  • clear() empties all current message lines from the log.
  • Disposing the console aborts all DOM event listeners, removes injected stylesheets, and clears the container element.

Remove or replace

Removing this module disables in-game chat and command console rendering. Dedicated servers and non-chat games operate normally without it. To replace it, author a presentation-tier module providing ui.chat@1.0.0 implementing ChatConsoleFactory.

Stability

Module version 0.1.1 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze. Visual styling adopts standard Spacelab UI theme CSS custom properties.

Example

Controller menu §

Editable local-player and controller settings UI.

What it does

This module renders the Players and Controllers tabs in the flight controls dialog. It provides the input.controller-menu service through the CONTROLLER_MENUS key. The Players tab configures local splitscreen multiplayer (1 to 4 seats), player names, input device assignments, and per-seat HUD navball locations. The Controllers tab configures stick deadzones, axis assignments and inversions, button mappings, and live hardware diagnostic meters. It operates gracefully without gamepad hardware, disabling live button capture while keeping layout editing fully functional. It does not advance simulation time or step physics worlds.

Provides and requires

  • Provides input.controller-menu@1.0.0 through the CONTROLLER_MENUS key: factory creating controller and local-player settings menus.
  • Requires input.local-pilots@^1.0.0 for player seat preference codecs and device assignments.
  • Requires input.flight-profiles@^1.0.0 for default controller layouts and profile normalization.
  • Requires input.flight-actions@^1.0.0 for action display labels.
  • Requires input.gamepad@^1.0.0 (optional) for live axis and button diagnostics and button capture; button names fall back to numbers when absent.

Settings

This module has no settings.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization resolves local pilot, flight profile, and flight action services, then provides the CONTROLLER_MENUS factory.
  • create mounts into a caller-provided root element and injects scoped CSS styles. If no gamepads manager is supplied, it creates and owns an internal FlightGamepad when input.gamepad is present.
  • open initializes tab views, synchronizes player selection, and starts a requestAnimationFrame loop to update live axis meters and button activity indicators.
  • Closing the dialog or pressing Escape cancels active button captures and halts the animation loop.
  • dispose detaches DOM markup, removes injected style elements, unbinds window listeners, and disposes any owned gamepad manager. Provider disposal closes all open menus with an AggregateError if cleanup fails.

Remove or replace

Games without local multiplayer or customizable controller layouts can omit this module. To replace it, implement a platform-tier module providing input.controller-menu@1.0.0 with your custom controls UI.

Stability

Module version 0.1.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze: menu options, services, and markup conventions may change before API freeze.

Example

examples/modules/controller-menu is a creator example implementing a minimal replacement provider for input.controller-menu@1.0.0 that manipulates local player preferences headlessly. Build and validate it from the repository root:

npm run module:build -- examples/modules/controller-menu/module.json /tmp/controller-menu-example.spacemod
npm run module:validate -- /tmp/controller-menu-example.spacemod

LAN discovery §

Borrowed text transports and explicit greeting codecs; no world dependencies.

What it does

This module scans local network addresses to discover active multiplayer game servers. It generates candidate endpoint lists across local subnet ranges, opens probe connections over a borrowed TransportProvider, reads the server's initial handshake greeting, and validates protocol identifier and universe model compatibility without importing world or physics logic.

Provides and requires

  • Provides net.lan-discovery@1.0.0 through the LAN_DISCOVERY key: a LanDiscoveryFactory instance.
  • Requires no services at initialization. Probing requires a caller-supplied TransportProvider (such as net.websocket).

Settings

  • timeoutMs (default 800, from 1 to 60000, step 1): Maximum wait for one candidate server to return a compatible greeting in milliseconds. A scan can explicitly override this default.
  • concurrency (default 32, from 1 to 64, step 1): Maximum number of candidate addresses probed together. Lower values reduce network load; higher values complete large scans sooner.
  • maxGreetingLength (default 16384, from 1 to 65536, step 1): Maximum UTF-16 characters accepted in a discovery greeting. Larger session messages are handled by the transport, not discovery.

Lifecycle

  • Initialization instantiates and provides the LanDiscoveryFactory configured with active module settings.
  • create(options) builds a dedicated LanDiscovery scanner bound to a transport, protocol ID, and greeting parser.
  • candidates(hints, port) generates candidate address lists from subnet hints and common private network prefixes.
  • probe(address, timeoutMs?, signal?) initiates a single handshake probe, closing the connection as soon as a greeting arrives or timeout expires.
  • scan(candidates, options) coordinates worker pools up to the concurrency limit, invoking onfound and onprogress callbacks.
  • Disposing the discovery instance or factory aborts all pending probe connections immediately.

Remove or replace

Removing this module disables automated LAN server scanning in multiplayer menus. Manual IP entry, tab discovery, and WebRTC invite codes remain operational. To replace it, author a module providing net.lan-discovery@1.0.0 implementing LanDiscoveryFactory.

Stability

Module version 0.1.0 uses API space-sim-game-api/2 and manifest format space-sim-module/2. Both are pre-freeze: discovery interfaces and candidate formats may be updated before release.

Example

examples/modules/discovery-lan demonstrates generating LAN candidate addresses and scanning endpoints with a mock transport. Build and validate it from the repository root:

npm run module:build -- examples/modules/discovery-lan/module.json /tmp/discovery-lan-example.spacemod
npm run module:validate -- /tmp/discovery-lan-example.spacemod
